By36.2 its common support includes Never for ALL players or for none.
If every z_i>0, pure deadline values along the increasing supported dates
converge to

    V_i(t) -> W_i+s_i*Z_i,    Z_i=product_(j!=i)z_j>0.

The strictly increasing function psi in(J4) and the uniform lower bound
(J5) imply that rho_i(t) has a limit r_i>0, with sum_i r_i=1. At the
Never atom(J4) gives tau*psi(rho_i(Never))=W_i-lambda_i. Consequently

    psi(r_i)=psi(rho_i(Never))+s_i*Z_i/tau.

Thus r_i>=rho_i(Never) for every i, strictly for one. Both vectors sum
to1, a contradiction. The convergence statement also follows directly
from bounded pure-response values by dominated convergence; it does not
identify finite escape with literal Never.

It remains to exclude infinite support with ALL laws proper on K10.
Here V_i(t)->W_i. By the same first-order conditions,

    rho_i(t)->r_i>0,  sum_i r_i=1,
    tau*psi(r_i)=W_i-lambda_i.                     (J9)

These density limits force conditional tail laws to become identical in
TOTAL VARIATION, not just weakly. Indeed put m=(1/4)sum_i p_i and
M_t=sum_(k>=t)m(k). At every supported finite k, p_i(k)=4*rho_i(k)*m(k).
Uniform convergence rho_i(k)->r_i over k>=t shows

    p_i(. | T_i>=t) - m(. | T>=t) ->0 in TV.        (J10)

All these tails are proper. Formula(J10) follows by dividing both
4*rho_i(k)*m(k) and its tail sum by4*r_i*M_t; r_i>0 keeps the division
uniform. Independence is preserved when conditioning all opponents on
survival to t, because that event is a product of their individual events.

For ANY one proper common clock law, its Never-response value N is at
least13/10. In fact the polynomial from36.3 satisfies

    B(q)>=(13/10)*(1-(1-q)^3)

for every q, by dN>=0. Summing the three-opponent survival telescope gives
the bound, since their proper tail survival tends to0. If its initial
hazard is q, the full conditional Never value is therefore at least
B(q)+(1-q)^3*(13/10), while immediate Quit has value Q(q). Their gap is
uniformly positive:

    B(q)+(13/10)*(1-q)^3-Q(q)
       =3/10+q*(21+9*q-23*q^2)/10 >=3/10.          (J11)

The last quadratic is concave with endpoint values21 and7, hence positive.
When q=1 the displayed inequality is direct; no conditioning on a zero-
probability remaining tail is needed. Missing dates before the first atom
are also permitted. This is a statement about pure current Quit versus
literal Never, not about a common profile's prescribed value.

Apply(J11) to the proper reference law m(. | T>=t). By(J10) the actual
three independent opponent tails are at vanishing product-TV distance
from three copies of that reference. Both immediate Quit and Never are
bounded measurable outcome functions, with absolute rewards at most10.
Their conditional values therefore differ from the reference values by
o(1), uniformly over the four recipients. Restoring the exact opponent
reach Z_i(t)=product_(j!=i)Pr(T_j>=t)>0 gives, for every sufficiently late
SUPPORTED finite date,

    W_i-V_i(t)>0       for EVERY i.                 (J12)

Earlier opponent-absorption payoffs cancel in this difference. The
vanishing TV error is conditional before multiplication by Z_i(t), so
there is no division of an uncontrolled unconditional error by small reach.

Finally(J4),(J9),(J12) imply psi(rho_i(t))<psi(r_i) for ALL four i at
the same supported date. Strict monotonicity gives rho_i(t)<r_i for all i,
contradicting that both vectors sum to1. This excludes every infinite-
support proper source. Combined with the positive-Never case it proves
the theorem at every tau>0.

The theorem does NOT exclude finite common support, whether its Never
atom is positive or zero. In particular a sequence of finite asymmetric
components can change supports with temperature and still supply all
errors. Conversely there is no hidden infinite-calendar equilibrium arm
of this particular regularizer that can simply absorb the missing finite-
menu selection. Full-source existence on K10 is exactly a FINITE-support
existence question once the above theorem is granted. Its quantifier over
all finite supports has not been settled by a bounded search.

### 36.6 What the asymmetric source attempt has and has not supplied

Splitting ANY common atom into two consecutive atoms in the SAME
proportion for all players leaves every K_i unchanged exactly: each
new atom has the old density ratio, and their masses sum to the old mass.
This includes splitting the Never atom into a last finite date and Never.
It is not an equilibrium-preserving move. Each unilateral split changes
the ratios against opponents' fixed choices, and its entropy charge is
scaled by own mass, not by joint game reach. Conditional tail games thus
have player-specific effective temperature tau divided by OPPONENT
survival. There is no unproved legal root-synthesis step hidden in the
cost-invariance observation.

A bounded floating-point Newton diagnostic solved the positive-support
finite-menu stationarity equations on a four-date menu at several
temperatures. Every converged sample was the known identical-clock
component; attempted proper-support solves on menus of sizes3,5,8,12
found no component. Neither statement is a uniqueness, nonexistence or
lower-bound certificate. Boundary components and other supports remain
free, and no decimal output is used in36.5.

The serious outstanding source assertion is now: for arbitrarily small
tau, does SOME asymmetric finite common-support component satisfy ALL
original late inequalities? Increasing the menu, selecting only one
branch, or preserving a cap-neutral ancestry is not justified. The exact
thirteen-date K10 profile with original full gap below1/300 shows that
asymmetric JOINT approximation is available at one accuracy; it is not
an exact overlap-source component or an all-errors producer. The next
global selection argument must add leverage beyond the finite concave-
game existence proof, or this regularizer should be parked as an exact-
source hypothesis with no established production advantage.

A bounded later overlap lookup found the SAME common-mixture cost in
`CODEX_BROUWER__NONBIJECTIVE_SINGLETON_SOURCE`, subsection DR3. That
existing note already states its bounded full-response endpoint and shows
that it does not repair increasing density-box approximations. DR4 also
excludes automatic better-reply security after common calendar escape.
Those facts are not new results here and do not exclude the entire FULL
regularized correspondence. The present distinct calculation is the
K10 restriction36.5, not introducing the cost or re-proving that endpoint.

## 37. Entire discounted source: fixed-rate compactness versus global selection

Status: ordinary UNREVIEWED mathematics. This is source analysis, not a new
UE reward class, a claimed arbitrary-table producer, or an exclusion of the
whole discounted selector. Section23.4 supplies a checked good component on
the already-solved Solan calibration; it remains a positive guardrail.

### 37.1 Exact question and the first objective falsifier

Keep the complete private-clock game and D_alpha of23.1. Every unilateral
replacement law, including every finite deadline and Never, is allowed.
For arbitrary Fin4 data the live question remains

    liminf_(alpha up to1) min/inf_(p in D_alpha) E_original(p)=0.       (F1)

No calendar, support, prescribed payoff or source ancestry is fixed across
accuracies. Existence of D_alpha is automatic. Requiring every deleted
opponent to absorb on the discount-negligible scale is only a sufficient
strategy restriction, not a necessary game semantic.

Maximizing joint discounted absorption alone does NOT select a good law.
The minimal two-player example has

    r({0})=(1,0), r({1})=(2,1), r({0,1})=(0,0), Never=0.

Fix alpha in(0,1) and an integer L with 2*alpha^L<=1. Player0 Quit0 and
player1 QuitL form exact discounted Nash. Player1 receives0 under every
replacement. Player0's deadlines1,...,L-1 give alpha^t<1, deadlineL gives0,
and every later deadline or Never gives2*alpha^L<=1. Thus the discounted
cap is1. Joint discounted absorption is1, the largest possible value,
while ORIGINAL player0's Never response earns2 instead of1. The equally
fast profile(Quit0,Never) is exact ORIGINAL Nash. Hence this example defeats
arbitrary selection among speed maximizers, not the best-component source
in(F1), nor a cap-sensitive tie breaker. It is not a UE obstruction.

### 37.2 A complete fixed-discount source theorem

Let there be finitely many n>=2 players, |r_i(S)|<=M and all own singleton
rewards s_i>0. Assume there is NO product binary Nash root with a sure
quitter, for ANY bounded continuation vector w in[-M,M]^n. A root is tested
against both Quit and Continue for every player, with actual independent
coins and w paid only if all coins Continue. This is an explicit raw-table
hypothesis, not an assumed property of a selected discounted branch.

Then, for each FIXED alpha in(0,1):

(a) D_alpha is nonempty compact in the product weak topology of probability
laws on X=Nat union{Never}, with its one-point compactification topology.

(b) Every p in D_alpha has at least two proper labels. No individual clock
has finite proper support, and every finite live date is reached positively.

(c) There is an integer N_alpha>=1 such that, simultaneously for EVERY
p in D_alpha, EVERY player i and EVERY date t,

    Pr_p(T_-i>=t+N_alpha | T_-i>=t)<=1/2.                         (F2)

Consequently E_p[T_-i+1]<=2*N_alpha. The bound is independent of the chosen
equilibrium component at this fixed discount, but may depend arbitrarily
badly on alpha and the table.

(d) Original terminal payoffs and unrestricted full response caps are
continuous on D_alpha. In particular min_(p in D_alpha) E_original(p)
is attained. All original finite deadlines AND Never enter this statement.

Proof of(a). Nonemptiness is Fink's stationary source already recorded
in23.1; it does not restrict the rest of D_alpha. The discounted terminal
kernel is continuous on X^n: near AllNever its magnitude is bounded by
M*alpha^(minimum finite clock), and finite first dates are isolated.
Every pure replacement payoff and prescribed discounted payoff is therefore
continuous in the product laws. The Nash inequalities define a closed
subset of the compact product mixed-strategy space, so D_alpha is compact.

Proof of(b). At the first possible sure-quitting date, all individual
survivals are positive. Exact discounted Nash gives a binary Nash root
against the actual bounded discounted continuation (arbitrary extensions
after zero-reach individual histories cause no difficulty). This contradicts
the raw no-sure hypothesis. Therefore no such date exists and all finite
survivals are positive.

Write z_j=Pr(T_j=Never). Fix i with Z_-i=product_(j!=i) z_j>0. Let V_i^alpha(t)
be its pure deadline payoff and W_i^alpha its Never payoff. Dividing their
difference by alpha^t gives the current Quit payoff on the event all opponents
survive to t, minus their discounted absorption reward at dates>=t. As t
tends to infinity, the first term tends to s_i*Z_-i and the absolute value
of the second is at most M*Pr(t<=T_-i<Never), which tends to0. Thus

    alpha^(-t)*(V_i^alpha(t)-W_i^alpha) -> s_i*Z_-i>0.

Also V_i^alpha(t)->W_i^alpha. Some finite deadline strictly exceeds Never,
and all sufficiently late deadlines lie below that fixed improvement.
The complete best-response set is consequently a nonempty FINITE set of
finite dates. Nash requires p_i supported on that set: i is finite proper,
contradicting the first paragraph. Hence Z_-i=0 for every i. This is exactly
the assertion that at least two labels are proper, not that all n are proper.

Proof of(c). The continuous functions

    f_(i,N)(p)=product_(j!=i) Pr_p(T_j>=N)

decrease to Z_-i=0 pointwise on compact D_alpha. The elementary decreasing-
compact-cover argument makes this convergence uniform. Choose one N_alpha
with every f_(i,N_alpha)<=1/2. Each reached conditional tail is AGAIN an
exact Nash law in the SAME D_alpha: copy a player's own original prefix
and replace its tail; the discounted gain is alpha^t times positive joint
reach times the conditional gain. Product conditioning preserves independence.
Applying the uniform bound to that tail gives(F2), with no error/reach
division or changed discount. Iteration gives survival at k*N_alpha at most
2^(-k), and summing blocks proves the expectation bound.

Proof of(d). Joint absorption tails are bounded by any deleted-opponent
survival tail, uniformly over D_alpha. Thus finite-prefix prescribed payoff
approximations converge uniformly. A response deadline later than H, or
Never, agrees in payoff with deadline H unless no opponent quits before H;
their payoff difference has absolute value at most

    2*M*sup_(p in D_alpha) Pr_p(T_-i>=H) ->0.

So the full cap is uniformly approximated by the maximum of finitely many
finite-deadline payoffs. Those are continuous. Prescribed payoffs, all full
caps, and their maximum debt are continuous, proving attained minimization.
No unlabelled weak-order compactification is used: the finite deadline
labels and their unilateral payoffs remain explicit.

### 37.3 Exact application to K10 and the unresolved quantitative step

The raw K10 table is36.3's table: own singleton1/opposite4/other0;
adjacent pair donor-1/recipient2/outsiders1; opposite-pair members0/outsiders1;
triple members-1/outsider10; grand9; Never0.

Here is the whole no-sure-root calculation, independent of continuation.
Rotate a putative sure quitter to0. Player2's Join-minus-Continue gains,
as the free opponent coins1,3 select none/one/both, are respectively-4,-2,-1.
It strictly Continues. With q2=0, player3's gains are-1 or-2, so it Continues.
Player1 then joins for2 rather than0, hence surely Quits. Owner0 now earns-1
instead of0 by withdrawing. Contradiction. Thus37.2 applies literally to
the ENTIRE K10 discounted correspondence.

The new fixed-alpha compactness fact does NOT make its window discount-
negligible. On the symmetric stationary branch put lambda=1-alpha and

    Q(q)=1-2q-2q^2+12q^3, B(q)=4q-5q^2+11q^3.

The exact interior discounted indifference equation is

    Q(q)*(1-alpha*(1-q)^3)-B(q)=0.                              (F3)

At(lambda,q)=(0,0), its derivatives are1 and-1. Ordinary IFT gives a
positive stationary source q=lambda+O(lambda^2). The discounted Bellman
telescope verifies all complete responses. Deleted-opponent survival over
N dates is(1-q)^(3N); therefore any uniform window in(F2) satisfies

    liminf_(lambda down to0) lambda*N_alpha >= (log2)/3.

Its original stationary prescribed payoff tends to5/4, while Never tends
to4/3. This is one bad branch, NOT a whole-correspondence lower bound. It
shows why applying the fixed-alpha bound uniformly over ALL components
cannot close(F1). Actual best-component selection must change the global
clock pattern, or use a sharper signed response-loss account.

The unconditional facts here remove an existence/tightness ambiguity from
the discounted source, but not its decisive deficit. The genuinely missing
mathematics is a global operation on the whole compact correspondence that
forces its ATTAINED minimum original full debt to approach0 as alpha approaches1,
or an invariant showing that every component keeps positive debt. Neither
fastest absorption, compactness, a stationary index, nor the first-order
small-hazard expansion supplies that operation. No new audit/export is
requested for this supporting source analysis.

Named sources reopened for this question:
`realizedAction_discountedPayoff_update_deviationAfterHistory` and
`realizedAction_afterHistoryProfile_isDiscountedNash_of_mem_support`,
`UniformEquilibrium/ProofView/Concepts/Stochastic/Equilibrium/DiscountedContinuation.lean`.
Their actual positive discounted reach identity is precisely the conditional
tail step above. Section23.1 lists the separately inspected Fink and quitting
discounted-payoff bridges. Noether's RM33 was read narrowly: its whole-source
obstruction fixes the period before taking the discount limit, and does not
exclude the unrestricted source(F1).

### 37.4 The first global enclosure test fails

A proposed shortcut was to prove that all viable discounted continuation
values lie in the unit box and then contract toward the small symmetric
branch. Even invariance of that box under the whole root operator is false.
At alpha=99/100 take the independent root

    q=(1/11,1/11,1/6,0)

and supplied bounded continuation vector

    v=(2/33,32/33,26/33,1) in[0,1]^4.

The effective one-step continuation is

    alpha*v=(3/50,24/25,39/50,99/100).

The three active players are exactly indifferent; their current payoffs are
Q0=2/3, Q1=49/66 and Q2=119/121. For player3, in opponent odds(1/10,1/10,1/5),
the Continue absorption numerator is47/100 and the all-Continue denominator
is363/250. Hence its Continue/current value is

    u3=(47/100+99/100)/(363/250)=365/363>1.

Its Quit value is317/363, so the inactive inequality holds strictly.
Thus this is a genuine full binary Nash root, not merely three owner
equations. At any alpha>491/500 the same active root and effective active
continuations give the same strict unit-box overshoot, after setting the
fourth next value to1. This does NOT assert that the input v is itself
in D_alpha, or give an actual discounted infinite clock component. It only
falsifies the proposed raw root-box invariance used in that global proof.

A bounded floating-point check of three-actor single-rotation periods and
full-support single-rotation periods found no additional sampled legal
component; the latter returned only the small symmetric stationary branch.
This is not an exhaustive root count, a calendar completeness theorem or
a whole-source obstruction. No fixed period or support pattern is retained
as a restriction in(F1). The failed enclosure and these diagnostics give
no license to discard macroscopic roots or arbitrary switching calendars.

### 37.5 A whole-correspondence Never-cap reduction

The following is ordinary UNREVIEWED supporting mathematics, not production
of a good component. Impose37.2's hypotheses and, additionally,

    r_i(S)>=0 whenever i is not in the nonempty coalition S.

For EVERY fixed alpha in(0,1), EVERY p in the ENTIRE D_alpha, EVERY player
i and EVERY reached date t, the original unrestricted best response is
Never. This assertion includes all finite deadlines, not only the support
of p_i. It applies to K10, whose passive rewards are0,1,4 or10.

Here is a direct proof and exact gap ledger. At a reached date write q_i
for its own hazard, L_i for opponent survival through the current root,
J=product_j(1-q_j), Q_i for its current Quit reward, and B_i for current
opponent absorption reward when it Continues. Write v_i,u_i,W_i for the
discounted prescribed, original prescribed and original Never values of
the conditional tail. A prime denotes the next conditional tail. All
these values are actual, and q_i<1 by37.2.

Discounted Never earns nonnegative reward, so v_i>=0 at EVERY tail. The
prescribed Bellman identities, with e_i=u_i-v_i, give

    e_i=J*(e_i'+(1-alpha)*v_i').

Joint termination makes the residual vanish on iteration. Consequently
e_i>=0. Since Continue is in support, discounted root Nash also gives

    v_i=B_i+alpha*L_i*v_i',          Q_i<=v_i.

Thus the original Continue value is

    B_i+L_i*u_i'=u_i+[q_i/(1-q_i)]*e_i >=u_i.

Iterating this inequality while holding i out, and using the actual deleted
termination of37.2, proves W_i>=u_i>=v_i>=Q_i. For any finite deadline h,
split its payoff at h. Replacing its terminal Q_i(h) by W_i(h) only raises
the payoff, and the resulting value is precisely Never. Therefore its full
original cap is W_i, as asserted. No unlabelled calendar transport or
deviation-class restriction has occurred.

In particular the source question(F1) on this subclass is EXACTLY selection
of components for which max_i[W_i(p)-u_i(p)] tends to0. With d_i=W_i-u_i,
the same identities give the nonnegative, exact recurrence

    d_i(t)=L_i(t)*d_i(t+1)
           +[q_i(t)/(1-q_i(t))]*e_i(t).                    (F4)

Equivalently d_i(0) is the sum of the latter charges weighted by the actual
opponent survival to t. The residual again vanishes by deleted termination.
Hence an actual macroscopic own hazard at positive opponent reach forces
small e_i there in any low-original-gap discounted family. This uses own
and opponent reaches separately: replacing either by joint reach would
change the identity.

The charges in(F4) have one sign, but no global component-selection descent
has been proved. Conditional re-rooting keeps a law in D_alpha but may
increase a different player's d_i. The bad symmetric K10 branch of37.3
has exactly these Never caps. Thus this reduction removes the deadline
search for this ENTIRE correspondence; it neither asserts(F1), supplies
the required component, nor excludes unrestricted original UE families.

### 37.6 The whole minimum source needs only joint termination and lower caps

This is ordinary UNREVIEWED supporting mathematics. It broadens the actual
source of37.2, not its vanishing-discount conclusion. The latter remains
OPEN. Unlike37.2, the following theorem makes NO no-sure-root assumption,
no restriction on passive reward signs, and no demand that every deleted
opponent clock terminate. It applies to all finite tables with strictly
positive own singleton rewards.

Fix a nonempty finite player set, a bounded original reward table, live
and Never payoff0, and s_i=r_i({i})>0 for every i. Let X=Nat union{Never}
with its one-point compactification topology. For fixed alpha in(0,1),
let D_alpha contain ALL exact Nash profiles of the realized-absorption
discounted game, as independent probability laws on X. Every unilateral
complete private replacement law remains allowed. Write U(p), B(p) and
E(p)=max_i(B_i(p)-U_i(p)) for ORIGINAL undiscounted semantics.

Claim. For every fixed alpha:

1. D_alpha is nonempty compact.
2. Every component terminates JOINTLY almost surely. The joint survival
   probabilities tend to0 uniformly over the ENTIRE D_alpha. At every
   positively reached live tail there is the same uniform conditional
   joint-termination window.
3. U is continuous on D_alpha. Each FULL original cap B_i is lower
   semicontinuous on the WHOLE product law space, because s_i>=0.
4. E is lower semicontinuous on D_alpha, so its infimum is attained.

The source assertion(F1) therefore has an actual minimum for EVERY such
positive-singleton table, including components with sure roots and only
one proper clock. No uniform statement as alpha tends to1 is inferred.

Proof. The discounted pure-clock payoff kernel on X^n is continuous:
near any finite first date its coalition is determined by finitely many
clopen date events, while when all clocks go to Never its absolute value
is at most M*alpha^h once every date is at least h. Hence the mixed payoff
is jointly continuous. Nonemptiness is the unconditional discounted Nash
source already used in23.1 and37.2. The Nash set is closed in the compact
product of probability-law spaces, and is therefore compact.

Suppose a discounted Nash component had positive joint Never mass. By
independence every player's Never mass m_i would be positive. At every
finite date t the live event has positive probability, so its conditional
tail is again an exact discounted Nash profile. For each player,

    Pr(T_i<Never | T_i>=t) tends to0,
    q_i(t)=Pr(T_i=t | T_i>=t) tends to0.

Every conditional prescribed discounted payoff then tends to0 in absolute
value: finite absorption probability is at most the sum of the displayed
conditional finite masses. But Quit now has payoff Q_i(t) tending to s_i>0.
This contradicts tail Nash. Thus joint Never probability is0. This does NOT
say all or two individual clocks are proper.

For a fixed integer h let

    J_h(p)=product_i Pr_p(T_i>=h).

Each J_h is continuous, since its marginal tails are complements of finite
clopen date sets. The functions decrease pointwise to joint Never mass0
on compact D_alpha. Compactness gives uniform convergence to0 (equivalently,
apply the finite subcover argument to the increasing open sets J_h<epsilon).
Choose N_alpha with sup_(p in D_alpha) J_(N_alpha)(p)<=1/2. Every reached
conditional tail belongs to the SAME D_alpha, so its conditional joint
survival over N_alpha dates is also at most1/2. A zero-reach tail needs no
conditioning or asserted law.

The original prescribed payoff truncated to first absorption before h is
continuous in p, and its difference from U is bounded by M J_h(p).
Uniform joint termination therefore proves continuity of U on D_alpha.
This uses JOINT, not deleted-opponent, survival.

For any arbitrary opponent law profile, let f_i(h) be the original value
of the pure finite deadline h and W_i the original Never value. Each
f_i(h) is continuous in the opponent laws: only their finite dates through
h and the clopen remaining tail enter this response kernel. Directly
splitting at the opponents' first date gives

    lim_(h to infinity) f_i(h)
        =W_i+s_i*product_(j!=i) Pr(T_j=Never).             (F5)

The tie contribution is bounded by M times the probability that the
opponents' first date is h, which tends to0. Strictly earlier absorption
converges to W_i, and strict later survival gives the displayed singleton
correction. Because s_i>=0, Never is dominated by the limiting finite
deadlines. Consequently the FULL behavioral cap is exactly

    B_i=sup_(h in Nat) f_i(h),                            (F6)

even if no pure deadline attains it. Arbitrary response mixtures cannot
exceed this supremum, and Never has not been omitted from the agency class.
A supremum of continuous functions is lower semicontinuous. Therefore
E=max_i(B_i-U_i) is lower semicontinuous on compact D_alpha and attains
its minimum. This proves the claim.

The cap continuity conclusion of37.2 really was stronger than necessary.
The already-recorded37.1 example demonstrates the distinction without a
new fixture. For fixed alpha, p_L=(Quit0,QuitL) is discounted Nash for all
sufficiently large L, and tends weakly to p_infinity=(Quit0,Never).
All components absorb JOINTLY at date0. Their original player0 caps are
2 for every finite L but1 at p_infinity. Thus original full caps are NOT
continuous on D_alpha, while their downward jump is exactly compatible
with lower semicontinuity and attainment of the original regret minimum.

Narrow overlap/dependency check: the discounted tail step is the actual
`realizedAction_afterHistoryProfile_isDiscountedNash_of_mem_support` and
`realizedAction_discountedPayoff_update_deviationAfterHistory` in
`UniformEquilibrium/ProofView/Concepts/Stochastic/Equilibrium/DiscountedContinuation.lean`.
Their supported-live-history gain factor is positive wherever conditioning
is used. The earlier Fink/source and clock agency declarations are recorded
in23.1. A bounded search in the chosen root, stationary, continuation and
marked-calendar subtrees found no named lower-semicontinuous original-cap
minimum theorem; no Lean implementation of this argument is claimed.

This removes an artificial source-side requirement for(F1): full cap
CONTINUITY and universal deleted-opponent termination are not needed to
select a best component at a fixed discount. The main missing producer is
unchanged: choose components across alpha tending to1 whose attained
original full regret tends to0. Lower semicontinuity, joint tightness and
the bad/good fastest-absorption tie do not prove that selection.

The positive-own restriction is conjecture-facing by an EXISTENTIAL
counterexample reduction, not a pointwise WLOG transformation of the
discounted source. The actual declaration
`exists_finFour_no_uniformPayoff_iff_exists_singlePivot` in
`UniformEquilibrium/Diagnostics/Quitting/FinFourSinglePivotNormalization.lean`
produces from any Fin4 counterexample SOME canonical counterexample with
own singleton vector(1,0,0,0). The definition
`IsSinglePivotSingletonTable` in
`UniformEquilibrium/Quitting/Terminal/SinglePivotFiniteMenuSource.lean`
is literally that vector. If its positive terminal maximum-exploitability
infimum is g, raise
only the three nonpivot OWN singleton coordinates by epsilon, leaving
all other coordinates and Never0 unchanged. The uniform reward distance
is epsilon. The actual theorem
`abs_quittingTerminalExploitabilityInf_sub_le_of_reward_close` in
`UniformEquilibrium/Quitting/Terminal/TerminalExploitabilityRewardRobustness.lean`
gives a remaining gap at least g-2epsilon>0 when2epsilon<g. Thus any
Fin4 counterexample yields some counterexample with ALL own singletons
strictly positive. Equivalently, the same file's
`exists_uniformEquilibriumPayoff_of_arbitrarily_close_reward_tables`
would close the canonical table if all its positive-own perturbations
were solved. These declarations and their defining hypotheses were
reopened for this composition. No old payoff, chronology, cap, Nash
component, or D_alpha correspondence is asserted to survive it.

### 37.7 Exact finite-word test: annotations need not already be actual tails

Status: supporting finite experiment and elementary error lemma, ordinary
mathematics, not checked in Lean. K10 already has a uniform-equilibrium proof
by the cap-chamber construction. The experiment tests component selection,
not new UE coverage or arbitrary-table source completeness.

Use the literal K10 table of37.3 and the owned reproducer
`../experiments/CODEX_TURING_BOX__DISCOUNTED_ROOT_PREFIX.py`. At the physical
tail, players0,2 choose Never; players1,3 independently choose Quit0 with
probability1/2 and Never otherwise. Its actual original and discounted
payoff is(1/4,5/4,1/4,5/4), and its FULL original cap vector is
(1/2,5/2,1/2,5/2). Instead of pretending this tail realizes a desired
discounted value, supply the separate bounded ANNOTATION

    v_N=(1,11/10,2,11/10).

The annotation need not belong to D_alpha. At a backward construction step,
solve the binary root game with Continue continuation alpha*v_next. Among
the roots found, select the one maximizing the sum of the new annotated
values. The search enumerates nonsure supports of size2 and3 algebraically;
it attempts full-support Newton roots only when those searches find none.
It does NOT enumerate the entire binary Nash correspondence, including all
degenerate singleton roots. Consequently neither the selector nor the
search failure message is a whole-correspondence assertion.

For a root q define Q_i as the immediate Quit endpoint, b_i as the expected
current opponent-absorption reward when i Continues, L_i as the probability
all opponents Continue, and c as the probability everyone Continues. The
declared backward recursion is

    v_i(t)=q_i Q_i+(1-q_i)b_i+alpha*c*v_i(t+1).

The actual physical payoff and FULL cap use, independently,

    U_i(t)=q_i Q_i+(1-q_i)b_i+c*U_i(t+1),
    B_i(t)=max{Q_i,b_i+L_i*B_i(t+1)}.                  (F7)

Thus all clocks are independent private clocks, and the actual word is
read in REVERSE order from its backward construction. No public signal or
correlation is introduced. The physical half-13 tail, including literal
Never, is present after the word.

Here is the precise residual/tail bound used by the checker. It holds for
ANY finite root word, not only its numerical generator. Let

    rho_i(t)=max{Q_i,b_i+alpha*L_i*v_i(t+1)}-v_i(t),
    O_i(t)=product_(s<t) L_i(s), J_t=product_(s<t)c(s).

The first quantity is nonnegative because v_i(t) is the root's own mixed
expected value. Write B_i^alpha(pi), U_i^alpha(pi) for the full discounted
cap and prescribed value of the ACTUAL physical tail pi. Iterating the
two-endpoint cap recursion, using the Lipschitz inequality for max, gives

    (B_i^alpha(word*pi)-v_i(0))_+
      <=sum_(t<N) alpha^t O_i(t)rho_i(t)
        +alpha^N O_i(N)(B_i^alpha(pi)-v_i(N))_+.

Also the exact Bellman identity gives

    U_i^alpha(word*pi)-v_i(0)
      =alpha^N J_N[U_i^alpha(pi)-v_i(N)].             (F8)

These formulas control EVERY finite deadline and Never, not only local
root responses. If all rho_i(t)<=rho, their combination bounds full
discounted regret by

    rho/(1-alpha)+alpha^N O_i(N)*
      [(B_i^alpha(pi)-v_i(N))_+ +|U_i^alpha(pi)-v_i(N)|].

The opponent survival factor is essential: replacing it by joint survival
would not justify full unilateral agency. In the recorded runs all
annotations stay within[-10,10], and the physical tail has cap at most5/2;
the bracket is safely at most25.

There is a separate ORIGINAL tail-independence bound. For any two actual
independent tails pi,pi' and the SAME fixed word, with rewards bounded in
absolute value by M, (F7) implies

    |U_i(word*pi)-U_i(word*pi')|<=2M J_N,
    |B_i(word*pi)-B_i(word*pi')|<=2M O_i(N).

Hence their original maximum regrets differ by at most
4M*max_i O_i(N). This does not compare two differently selected words,
nor does it make the annotation an actual continuation.

Exact reproducible command (NumPy search followed by integer certification):

```text
python -u experiments/CODEX_TURING_BOX__DISCOUNTED_ROOT_PREFIX.py --alpha .9999 --steps 1000 --literal-seed --terminal 1 1.1 2 1.1 --selector sum --check-exact
```

The script SHA256 for these runs is
`2ddd6007da9cd20a5fce0192a27bb79144bd576776295bc8cb371cf683086fe0`.
Each searched hazard is rounded to an integer multiple of10^(-12).
The checker then reconstructs every coalition probability, annotation,
original payoff, and FULL cap with integers and common denominators.
The Bellman equality is separately reconstructed and checked exactly.
It verifies rho_i(t)<=10^(-10) by integer comparisons at EVERY root and
recipient; floating residuals below are descriptive, not certificates.

The exact conclusions of the displayed run are:

- original FULL maximum regret E<459/1000000<1/2000;
- every deleted prefix survival O_i(N)<10^(-80);
- binary root debt at most10^(-10), and Bellman arithmetic residual zero;
- actual full discounted regret at most10^(-6)+25*10^(-80);
- original maximum-regret dependence on changing the physical tail is at
  most40*10^(-80), for the fixed rounded word.

For diagnostic scale, the largest computed binary root debt is approximately
4.1251141555544305*10^(-12), the largest active Quit/Continue difference is
4.403722950360233*10^(-12), and the first number divided by1-alpha is
approximately4.125114155554885*10^(-8). The EXACT coarse bound uses10^(-10),
not those floating maxima.

Changing only the discount in the displayed command gives these additional
integer-checked finite tests; all use the SAME annotation, physical tail,
selector and1000 construction steps:

| alpha | exact original E upper bound | all O_i(N) below |
| --- | --- | --- |
| .99 | 32531/1000000 | 10^(-74) |
| .999 | 4147/1000000 | 10^(-79) |
| .9999 | 459/1000000 | 10^(-80) |

This three-point table is NOT an asymptotic theorem. No exact recurrent
word or arbitrarily accurate family has been proved. In particular, an
accurate finite discounted source is not silently called a member of
D_alpha, the EXACT discounted correspondence.

The clean contrasting control is

```text
python experiments/CODEX_TURING_BOX__DISCOUNTED_ROOT_PREFIX.py --alpha 0.999 --steps 200
```

With its ACTUAL symmetric stationary discounted seed and the default
sampled-root selector, it remains at original E approximately.0838721029
and hazards approximately.0009911124. Thus prepending the selected roots
does not automatically cure original regret. This control is a branch
failure, not an exclusion of every other root at that seed.

The mechanism evidence is that a bounded unrealized terminal annotation
can select macroscopic, changing-support words whose ACTUAL original
payoff and full caps forget the physical tail, while the small binary
residual also gives a complete approximate discounted-Nash guarantee.
The open producer question is what forces access to good such words for
arbitrary strictly positive-own Fin4 data as accuracy varies. Neither the
sum selector, the arbitrary annotation, finite-error certification, nor
fixed-discount compactness supplies that global argument.

### 37.8 The exact/approximate-source fork and a one-sided cap ledger

Status: supporting ordinary mathematics, independently unreviewed. No new
UE reward class or complete producer is asserted. The ENTIRE exact source
question(F1) is still open. The finite words in37.7 neither establish it
nor demonstrate that exact discounted equilibria are an inadequate source.

Here are two one-sided comparisons that apply to arbitrary finite tables,
not only the passive-nonnegative K10 table. Fix a player i with own
singleton s_i>=0, rewards bounded in absolute value by M, and alpha in(0,1).
Write lambda=1-alpha. Against ANY fixed independent opponent clocks let
B_i be the full original cap and C_i^alpha the full discounted cap. Then

    C_i^alpha<=B_i+M lambda.                         (F9)

To check every deadline and Never, let h_t be the unconditional probability
that the FIRST opponent absorption is at t, a_t its expected unjoined
reward contribution, and A_t=sum_(u<=t)a_u. The original Quit-k payoff is
f_k=sum_(t<k)a_t+e_k, where e_k includes the joined reward at k and own
singleton on opponent survival. The original Quit-(t+1) comparison and
s_i>=0 give

    A_t<=f_(t+1)+M h_(t+1)<=B_i+M h_(t+1).

The literal Abel identity for the discounted Quit-k payoff is

    g_k=alpha^k f_k+lambda sum_(t<k)alpha^t A_t.

Its coefficients of B_i sum to1, and sum_t h_t<=1. Hence
g_k<=B_i+M lambda. For Never the corresponding identity is
g_Never=lambda sum_(t>=0)alpha^t A_t, with the same bound.
This proves(F9) for all complete private replacements. It is one-sided:
late positive rewards can make B_i-C_i^alpha large.

There is a second bound for EVERY actual discounted Nash profile p:

    U_i(p)>=v_i^alpha(p)-M lambda.                    (F10)

No nonnegative passive-reward assumption is used. At a date t with
positive original joint survival J_t, conditional tails remain discounted
Nash, because a player may preserve its pre-t law and replace its own
conditional tail. If L_i(t) is the current opponent Continue probability,
its actual normalized discounted continuation value obeys

    v_i(t)>=Q_i(t)>=-M[1-L_i(t)].

The first inequality is the COMPLETE immediate-Quit deviation, and the
second uses only s_i>=0. Absolute summation of actual absorption rewards
gives the exact identity

    U_i(p)-v_i(0)=lambda sum_(t>=1) J_t v_i(t).

Since J_t is at most the opponent survival O_i(t), and
sum_t O_i(t)[1-L_i(t)]<=1, the negative part is at most M lambda.
Zero-reach tails contribute zero, so no sequential optimality is asserted
there. Literal Never contributes zero to both payoff sums.

In particular, for every player of a nonnegative-own table and every
member of the ENTIRE D_alpha,

    B_i-v_i^alpha>=-M lambda,
    0<=B_i-U_i<=B_i-v_i^alpha+M lambda.

Thus producing a component with small positive cap defect
max_i(B_i-v_i^alpha)_+ would suffice. This isolates a useful objective but
does NOT produce its minimizer tending to0. A root-prefix minimization
test still encounters the all-Continue barrier: if continuation values
are above own singletons, a nonzero improving root is not forced.
No universal descent or implication from standard-Q is claimed.

There is also an exact FIXED-alpha comparison between the exact and
approximate source objectives. Assume all own singletons are positive,
and use37.6. For eta>=0 let

    m_alpha(eta)=inf{E_original(p): E_alpha(p)<=eta},

where BOTH defects use all complete unilateral replacements and the
profile ranges over all independent complete clocks. Then

    lim_(eta down0) m_alpha(eta)=m_alpha(0).            (F11)

Indeed a near-minimizing sequence with eta down0 has a subsequence in the
compact product of one-point-compactified clock-law spaces. Discounted
payoff and cap are continuous, so its limit lies in D_alpha. That limit
has joint Never mass0 by37.6. At such a limit the original prescribed
payoff is continuous: finite-prefix truncation is continuous and the
remaining payoff is at most M times joint survival. The original full
cap is lower semicontinuous, including Never, by the nonnegative-own
finite-deadline supremum formula. Hence E_original at the limiting exact
component is at most the lower limit of the candidate errors. The reverse
inequality follows because every exact component is eta-admissible.
This does not require two-sided cap continuity or uniform deleted
termination. Infima over the approximate sets need not be attained.

The order of limits is essential. Equation(F11) is NOT a uniform modulus
as alpha tends to1. It therefore does not validate replacing the exact
source by errors merely o(1-alpha), nor does it turn a single finite-word
certificate into an exact D_alpha component. At a FIXED alpha, however,
arbitrarily accurate words whose ORIGINAL errors remain bounded by e
would yield an exact component with original error at most e, by the
displayed argument. Unrealized bounded annotations are not by themselves
evidence that a larger approximate-source family is required.

The live fork is consequently precise: either force such original-good
words while taking their discounted residual to0 at each fixed alpha,
or exploit a genuinely joint alpha/residual family whose original caps
are controlled directly without claiming exact-source membership. Both
remain allowed. Neither fixed-alpha compactness, the new one-sided
ledger, nor the finite numerical branch currently chooses between them
or establishes arbitrary-data branch access.

### 37.9 All-player proper envelopes lose an indispensable one-owner exit

Status: elementary ordinary UNREVIEWED method falsifier. This disposes of
the proposed source below; no new UE reward class or original-game
nonexistence is asserted.

For arbitrary asymmetric rates delta_i in(0,1), consider the independent
private domains

    K_(delta_i)={p_i: Pr(T_i>=t, including Never)
                       <=(1-delta_i)^t for every finite t>=0}.

An auxiliary source would be ANY exact Nash component of the ORIGINAL
terminal game on the product of these domains. Original regret still
permits every complete deadline/Never replacement. The hoped-for selector
was the infimum of original regret over ALL such components and ALL rate
vectors tending coordinatewise to0, with no symmetry restriction. Each
K_(delta_i) forces its private clock to be proper. This differs from30.1's
C_delta, which exempts Never and bounds only the finite tail; its old
whole-source theorem is not being transferred by set inclusion.

The entire proposed selector fails on the following literal FOUR-player
table. For every nonempty quitter coalition S set

    r_i(S)=1 if S={i};
    r_i(S)=0 if i belongs to S and |S|>=2;
    r_i(S)=2 if i does not belong to S;
    live/Never=0.

In fact EVERY independent complete profile with at least TWO proper
labels has full cap B_i=2 for EVERY player. At least one opponent of each
i is proper, so literal Never earns2 almost surely. No outcome reward
exceeds2, proving the upper bound as well. Joint absorption is certain.
At a singleton terminal coalition the total reward is7; at a coalition
of size k>=2 it is2(4-k)<=4. Hence

    sum_i U_i<=7,
    sum_i(B_i-U_i)>=1,
    E_original=max_i(B_i-U_i)>=1/4.

This applies to EVERY feasible profile in every product of K_(delta_i),
therefore to ALL auxiliary Nash components for EVERY positive asymmetric
rate vector, independently of its limit order. It is stronger than a bad
selected component and does not need a source-existence assertion.

Nevertheless fix any owner j, prescribe its sure Quit0, and prescribe
Never to all others. The owner earns1, while any complete response against
all-Never opponents earns at most1. Every outsider earns2, the largest
reward in the table. This is an exact unrestricted terminal Nash profile.
It is also an exact discounted Nash profile for EVERY alpha in(0,1):
absorption is at0, the owner can only lower its own singleton payoff by
delay, and outsider deviations cannot exceed2. Thus the ENTIRE original
discounted source passes this calibration by retaining one proper label.

The source is retired, not repaired by different rates or another
component search. The general lesson is the mathematical restriction:
requiring two proper prescribed labels needs an alternative arm admitting
one proper label. Properness of ALL clocks is not a harmless approximation
to selective Never, even though proper laws are weakly dense and all own
singletons here are strictly positive. No analogy with the shared joint-
survival constraint is used; that constraint can retain selective Never.
The live arbitrary-table question remains37.8's good-component/actual-word
selection with the complete original caps, not an all-proper substitute.

## 38. Sink-or-receiver escape for a two-ledger cap chamber

Status: independently reviewed ordinary mathematics, exported as
[`Sink-or-receiver cap chamber`](../exports/SINK_OR_RECEIVER_CAP_CHAMBER_UNIFORM_EQUILIBRIUM.md).
Both independent reviews are in the feedback files linked near the top.
This changes a raw
reward guard of the reviewed two-ledger theorem, not merely the description
of a supplied source. Nothing below is claimed implemented in Lean. The
finite-game boundary argument develops Nash's Section38 weak-face lemma;
its direct proof is included, so this section does not depend on an
unwritten index theorem or another conference note for its truth.

### 38.1 Finite data, graph convention and exact theorem

Let I be any finite player set with at least two elements. For every
nonempty S subset I let r(S) be a finite real reward vector. Live and
Never rewards are0. Randomization is private and independent, and a
deviation replaces one player's ENTIRE behavioral law. Put s_i=r_i({i})
and assume s_i>=0. Fix nonzero weights a_i>=0 and define, for every
nonempty proper S,

    H_a(S)=sum_i a_i[r_i(S)-s_i],
    J_a(S)=sum_(i notin S) a_i[r_i(S union{i})-r_i(S)].

Assume:

1. H_a(S)>=0 for every nonempty proper S, and H_a({j})>0 for some j.
2. J_a(S)<=0 for every nonempty proper S.
3. Every nonempty proper A contains i such that r_i(S)<=s_i for EVERY
   nonempty S subset A containing i.
4. In EVERY nonempty proper induced subset L, the graph

       j -> i  iff  i!=j and r_i({i,j})>r_i({j})

   has a sink OR a vertex receiving an arrow from every other vertex
   of L. A sink has NO outgoing positive arrow within L. A universal
   recipient has ALL the stated incoming arrows, strictly positive;
   no outgoing-arrow assumption is imposed on that vertex.

THEOREM. These raw guards imply a uniform-equilibrium payoff in the
original game against all unrestricted behavioral replacements. There
is ONE target v such that every positive accuracy has a profile and a
horizon threshold, after which all expected average payoffs approximate
v and every complete unilateral replacement earns at most v_i plus the
accuracy. There is no supplied profile, continuation, root selection,
carrier, convergence rate, chronology or public correlation.

FIN4 COROLLARY. For four players guard4 is equivalent to: NO proper
triple induces EXACTLY a directed triangle, with its three reverse
arrows absent. Thus two-cycles and triangles with ANY additional
positive reverse chord are allowed. The old acyclicity guard is strictly
stronger, as the exact witness in38.5 shows. This is not a claim allowing
all graphs, nor a necessary condition for existence of UE.

To check the graph equivalence, sets of size1 have sinks. A two-vertex
set either has a sink or is a two-cycle, whose vertices both receive
the other arrow. On three vertices, if there is no sink and no universal
recipient, every outdegree is at least1 and every indegree at most1.
There are therefore exactly three arrows and every degree equals1;
without loops this is exactly a directed triangle. Conversely that
triangle has neither a sink nor a universal recipient. Zero and negative
insertion differences simply mean an absent arrow.

### 38.2 Full finite-game escape, including inactive players

LEMMA. In the finite Boolean root game, let all-Continue payoff B satisfy
B>=s and let L={i:B_i=s_i} be nonempty. If the induced graph on L has
a sink or a universal recipient, the game has a nonzero FULL mixed Nash
root. No sign restriction on other finite rewards is needed.

For a sink i, let only i quit with probability p>0. It is indifferent,
since its two endpoints are s_i=B_i. For every other player j,

    Continue_j-Quit_j
      =(1-p)(B_j-s_j)+p[r_j({i})-r_j({i,j})].       (G1)

This is nonnegative on L by the sink property. It is nonnegative outside
L for all sufficiently small p>0 because B_j>s_j; finitely many strict
inequalities are retained simultaneously. Hence this is a FULL root,
not merely an owner-subgame root.

For a universal recipient i, suppose for contradiction that all Continue
is the ONLY Nash root. Perturb the finite game by adding epsilon>0 to
player i's payoff whenever IT chooses Quit, without changing any other
payoff or the all-Continue vector. Choose any Nash root q^epsilon of the
perturbed finite game. Finite mixed Nash existence applies to every
epsilon. Compactness of the product cube and the closed Nash graph imply
q^epsilon->0: every convergent subsequence as epsilon->0 has an original
Nash limit, which by assumption is0.

Every j outside L strictly prefers Continue at q=0, since s_j-B_j<0.
Continuity therefore forces q_j^epsilon=0 for all sufficiently small
epsilon. Delete those coordinates. The ORIGINAL forced-Quit minus
forced-Continue difference of player i has the finite multiaffine
expansion

    g_i(q)=sum_(j in L minus{i}) h_ij q_j+O(t^2),
    h_ij=r_i({i,j})-r_i({j})>0,
    t=sum_(j in L minus{i}) q_j.                       (G2)

The constant term is0 because B_i=s_i, and own q_i never enters its
endpoint difference. The remainder bound is uniform in a neighborhood
of0; it follows directly by expanding finitely many opponent-coalition
probabilities. If L has another vertex, put h=min h_ij>0 and bound the
remainder by Kt^2. Then g_i(q)>=h t-Kt^2>=0 for sufficiently small t.
If L={i}, g_i(q)=0 exactly. In BOTH cases the perturbed difference is
g_i(q^epsilon)+epsilon>0, so Nash optimality forces q_i^epsilon=1.
This contradicts q^epsilon->0. A nonzero ORIGINAL full root exists.

No limit of the perturbed roots is declared nonzero. The contradiction
shows that the original finite correspondence already contains a distant
nonzero root; continuity of a root selector is neither proved nor used.
The perturbation is a proof inside the finite root game, not a payment
or correlated device added to the original quitting game.

Connection with Nash's weak-face lemma: if all Continue were unique,
that lemma supplies a probability vector x on L with (-h)x>=0, with
h_ii=0. When there is no sink but i is a universal recipient, its row
forces x to be concentrated on i. Since i is not a sink, some outgoing
arrow i->j makes the j row negative, contradiction. This recovers exactly
the graph consequence needed here. The direct one-player perturbation
above supplies a self-contained proof and also handles a receiver that
is itself a sink. This is a finite-game application/variant of that
boundary escape, not a new general index theorem.

A sharp finite-game guardrail is the following three-player Boolean game,
not a UE counterexample. All Continue and own singletons pay0. At sole j,
recipient j+1 gets-2 and recipient j-1 gets0, modulo3. Every participant
in a pair or the grand coalition gets-1; a pair's passive recipient gets0.
For each i the root difference is exactly

    g_i=q_(i-1)-q_(i+1)-q_(i-1)q_(i+1).

The graph is a bare directed triangle and ALL participants are flat.
Nevertheless all Continue is its unique Nash root: one active label gives
its inactive successor a positive gap; two active labels give one of them
a strictly negative gap; with all three active the sum of the three gaps
is minus the positive sum of pair products, whereas supported Nash would
make every gap nonnegative. Thus flatness alone cannot replace guard4.
This finite-game example does not assert failure of the other chamber
guards, nonexistence of quitting-game UE, or completeness of this graph
criterion.

### 38.3 Actual full-cap carrier, seed and root ledgers

For an actual independent private-clock profile let U_i be its terminal
payoff and B_i the supremum over ALL complete replacements. Affinity in
the replacement law makes this the supremum over every finite pure
deadline and literal Never. Let d_i=B_i-U_i>=0, E=max_i d_i, and

    L_a(B)=sum_i a_i(B_i-s_i).

The closure S_actual of all actual pairs(U,B) is compact in the finite
reward box and retains B>=U. It need not be convex, and a closure point
is NOT silently declared an actual profile.

For a fixed independent product root q, put

    c=product_i(1-q_i), alpha_i=product_(j!=i)(1-q_j),
    Q_i=E[r_i(A union{i})],
    b_i=E[r_i(A) 1_(A nonempty)],

where A is its opponent quitter set. Full prefix semantics is exactly

    U_i'=q_i Q_i+(1-q_i)(b_i+alpha_i U_i),
    B_i'=max{Q_i,b_i+alpha_i B_i}.                     (G3)

This continuous map preserves S_actual: it is actual prefixing on actual
profiles, followed by closure for a FIXED selected q. At a FULL cap-Nash
root, namely a Nash root of the finite game with continuation B,

    B'=cB+sum_(S nonempty) p_q(S)r(S),
    d_i'=c d_i,                         E'=cE.         (G4)

The zero-hazard players' Nash inequalities are essential to this exact
identity. If any q_i=1 then c=0 and (G4) gives a diagonal of S_actual,
which already supplies the conclusion.

The seed is produced from raw data. Choose j with H_a({j})>0, prescribe
sure Quit0 to j and Never to everyone else. Then

    U_i=r_i({j}), B_j=s_j,
    B_i=max{r_i({j}),r_i({i,j})} for i!=j.

For the owner every finite deadline pays s_j and Never0; for another
player Quit0 and any strictly positive deadline/Never give the two listed
rewards. Thus these are ACTUAL unrestricted caps. By B>=U and a>=0,
L_a(B)>=H_a({j})>0, while min(B-s)<=0. If E=0 the seed solves the game.
Otherwise define lambda=L_a(B)/E>0 and the nonempty compact chamber

    C={(U,B) in S_actual: min_i(B_i-s_i)<=0,
                               L_a(B)>=lambda E}.     (G5)

There is no global B>=0 assertion. At a positive-E chamber point a root
with all q_i in(0,1) would give, by active indifference and z_i=q_i/(1-q_i),

    B_i-s_i=sum_(empty!=A subset I minus{i}) z_A
                       [r_i(A union{i})-r_i(A)],
    L_a(B)=sum_(empty!=S proper subset I) z_S J_a(S)<=0. (G6)

But L_a(B)>=lambda E>0. Every full cap-Nash root at such a point therefore
has proper support, unless a sure probability already gives the diagonal.
For nonempty proper support A, guard3 gives i in A whose forced-Quit
endpoint Q_i<=s_i. Supported Nash optimality makes B_i'=Q_i, preserving
the low face. Every nonempty root coalition is proper, so guards1 and
(G4) give

    L_a(B')=c L_a(B)+sum_(S nonempty) p_q(S)H_a(S)
              >=c L_a(B)>=lambda cE=lambda E'.         (G7)

Consequently EVERY full cap-Nash root preserves C at a positive-debt
point or immediately gives a diagonal. All Continue fixes the pair.
No correlation, target preservation or cross-accuracy chronology is used.

### 38.4 Compact minimum and fixed-target unrestricted conclusion

Suppose the attained minimum E* on C is positive, at(U*,B*). If some
B_i*<s_i, all Continue is not Nash; finite Nash existence gives a nonzero
full root. By(G4),(G7) it stays in C with smaller E, or gives a diagonal.
Both are contradictions.

Otherwise B*>=s. Its tied set L={i:B_i*=s_i} is nonempty by the low-face
condition and proper because L_a(B*)>=lambda E*>0. Guard4 and38.2 supply
a nonzero FULL original root. Once again(G4),(G7) contradict minimality:
c<1, and either E'=cE*<E* inside C or c=0 closes the game immediately.
Thus min_C E=0. At the minimizing carrier point B=U=v. Actual profiles
approximate this diagonal with terminal regret tending to0 and payoff
tending to the SAME v. The full-behavior diagonal consumer yields the
stated uniform conclusion with target selected before accuracy.

This is global CONSTRAINED minimization on an actually generated semantic
carrier, not minimization over a chosen root spine. No chronological
temporalization is required. The new principle is only finite-game escape
from the low tied face; the two different raw higher-coalition ledgers and
proper-support low-face preservation remain genuine hypotheses.

### 38.5 Exact four-player witness outside the old graph guard

Let I=Z/4 and a_i=1. On a singleton{j}, owner j gets1, opposite j+2 gets4,
and both other recipients get0. On adjacent pair{j,j+1}, donor j gets-1,
successor j+1 gets2, and both outsiders get3/2. On an opposite pair both
members get0 and both outsiders get2. On a triple all members get-1 and
the omitted player gets10. The grand row is(9,9,9,9). Make exactly TWO
exceptions:

    r_0({0,1})=1/2,            r_2({0,1,2})=2.          (G8)

Never0. Every unmentioned coordinate is fixed by the preceding rules.
The exact ledgers are

    H(singleton)=1;
    H(01)=3/2, H(other pairs)=0;
    H(012)=6, H(other triples)=3;
    J(1)=-3/2, J(other singletons)=-3;
    J(01)=-2, J(other adjacent pairs)=-5;
    J(opposite pairs)=-6, J(triples)=-1.

Every proper A has a flat participant. In an adjacent pair take its
donor, including donor0 at reward1/2<1 in01; in an opposite pair take
either member. For triples012,013,023,123 use respectively0,3,2,1.
Each such player has EVERY participant reward in that triple at most1,
including its subpairs. Singletons are flat by equality. The graph is
the Hamiltonian cycle0->1->2->3->0 PLUS1->0. No induced triple is a bare
directed triangle. The two-cycle0<->1 violates the OLD acyclicity guard.
Thus this table is admitted by38.1 and not by the old literal graph guard.

The actual sole-owner0 seed has

    U=(1,0,4,0), B=(1,2,4,0), E=2, L=3, lambda=3/2.

All H/J values above were independently recomputed in exact rational
arithmetic locally; that is an experiment checking the printed arithmetic,
not an independent proof review or Lean seal.

### 38.6 ENTIRE sure-owner and punishment-tail selections

For each sure owner, the following exhausts the FULL induced game of ALL
other players, including mixed and boundary choices. Continuation values
do not enter those players' endpoints because their sure opponent already
quits. Write each listed rate vector in increasing player order.

* Owner0: player3's joining-minus-Continue values against the four pure
  choices of players1,2 are(-1,-5/2,-3,-1), hence3 strictly Continues.
  The remaining two response gaps are

      g1=2-5q2,                g2=-4+(9/2)q1.

  Their unique Nash point is(q1,q2,q3)=(8/9,2/5,0); the opposite slopes
  exclude all four pure corners and force this interior intersection.
  The owner's forced-Quit payoff Q0=-1/45, absorbed Continue contribution
  b0=32/45 and empty-opponent mass alpha0=1/15. Hence even continuation
  B0=-1 gives Continue29/45>Q0. In particular every NONNEGATIVE
  punishment continuation fails the owner floor condition.
* Owner1: opposite3 has four strict negative gaps(-4,-5/2,-5/2,-1).
  Once3 Continues, g2=2-(3/2)q0>0, forcing2 sure; then
  g0=1/2-3q2<0. Unique induced(q0,q2,q3)=(0,1,0).
* Owner2: opposite0 has the same strict negative table. Once0 Continues,
  predecessor1 strictly Continues and successor3 surely Quits.
  Unique induced(q0,q1,q3)=(0,0,1).
* Owner3: opposite1 has the same strict negative table. Once1 Continues,
  predecessor2 strictly Continues and successor0 surely Quits.
  Unique induced(q0,q1,q2)=(1,0,0).

For owners1,2,3 the owner Quit value is-1, Continue contribution0 and
empty-opponent mass0. No continuation can repair these owner inequalities.
All passive rewards are nonnegative, so Never guarantees each player's
punishment lower value at least0. This excludes the ENTIRE singleton-base
induced-Nash punishment-floor compiler, not only one selected child.
It also excludes every full zero-tail Nash root with a sure label, hence
all persistent-base/free selections whose full leave/join tests furnish
such a root. A multi-owner punishment base has another sure opponent
for each deviator, so the tail is irrelevant and is excluded as well.

The quantifier is NOT arbitrary signed continuation: owner0 could be
supported with a sufficiently negative continuation. In particular no
blanket 'no sure Nash at every finite continuation' is being asserted.

### 38.7 Every universal quiet-child certificate fails

For every nonempty proper child C other than01 and013, choose j in C
with j+1 outside C and, if j=1, with0 outside C. Such a choice exists
by inspection of the four-cycle; the two exceptions are precisely where
the reverse insertion1->0 prevents the old singleton witness.
Sole-j-Quit0 with every other child player Never is exact unrestricted
child Nash. Its predecessor can join for-1 versus0, its opposite for0
versus4, and its favorable successor is absent; the new reverse edge
is excluded by the choice. All child debts and joint-Never mass are0.
Missing successor j+1 can Quit0 for2 instead of its prescribed0.

For C=01 or013, prescribe sure simultaneous Quit0 to0 and1, and Never
to3 if it is in C. The two owners receive1/2 and2 and can withdraw only
for0; child3 receives3/2 and could join only for-1. Thus this is exact
full child Nash with joint-Never mass0. Omitted2 can Quit0 for
r2(012)=2 instead of r2(01)=3/2, gain1/2.

Therefore EVERY child has an actual zero-debt, zero-Never profile whose
quiet full-game lift has a positive outsider debt. No finite-slack
universal inequality

    outsider debt <= nonnegative weighted child debts + rho*jointNever

can hold for that child, for ANY weights or finite rho. This excludes
all raw kinds whose checked conclusion is such a universal inequality,
including withdrawal/future/join and capped-clock certificates. It does
NOT exclude a new source permitted to choose ONLY another favorable
child equilibrium rather than certify every child profile.

### 38.8 No stationary exact Nash, over the entire support set

The sure-owner calculation excludes every stationary root with any sure
label: stationary continuation is an actual payoff in[-1,10], while
38.6 excludes all B0>=-1 and excludes the other owners without a bound.
All-Never fails since each own singleton is1. For any remaining stationary
exact Nash, all passive rewards are nonnegative, so its literal Never
response gives B_i=U_i>=0.

Full support is impossible. Every terminal coalition has nonnegative
unit-weight H, including grand H=32. Singleton terminal probabilities
are strictly positive, hence sum B-4>0. But full-support root identity(G6)
and all negative J give sum B-4<0. One-player support fails because its
successor prefers joining for2 to passive0. On opposite two-player
support, an active player's Never earns4 while its Quit endpoint is<=1.
On adjacent two-player support, the successor's Never is0 while its
Quit endpoint is1 plus its opponent hazard, strictly positive.

For support012 write q=(a,b,c,0), a,b,c in(0,1). The active endpoint
identities and U0>=0 imply

    Q0=1-b/2-c-bc/2, b0=c(4-(5/2)b),
    0<=Q0-b0=1-b/2-5c+2bc.

Thus c<=(1-b/2)/(5-2b)<=1/5. Player1 has
Q1=1+a-2c-ac>=3/5, whereas its Never payoff is
2ac/(a+c-ac)<=2c<=2/5. Exact stationary indifference of an active player
makes its value equal its Never payoff, contradiction.

For supports123 and230, rotate their three labels to(a,b,c). Neither
exception(G8) occurs in an active player's payoff. The first player's
identity is

    Q=1-2b-c+bc, b_abs=c(4-(5/2)b),
    0<=Q-b_abs=1-2b-5c+(7/2)bc.

It forces b<1/2 and c<=(1-2b)/(5-(7/2)b)<=1/5. The middle player again
has Quit>=3/5 and Never<=2/5. Finally for support301, let
(a,b,c)=(q3,q0,q1). The first player's identical inequality still gives
c<=1/5. Middle0 now has

    Q0=1+a-c/2-(5/2)ac>=9/10,
    Never0=2ac/(a+c-ac)<=2/5,

again impossible. Every support has been exhausted. Thus all sound raw
producers concluding an EXACT stationary product Nash fail on this table,
including all their parameter and support choices. This statement does
not exclude small stationary regret or nonstationary approximate families.

### 38.9 Bounded singleton, quotient, weight and template coverage

These are comparisons for the EXACT38.5 table, not for every member of
38.1 and not a claim of exhaustive novelty. The singleton matrix remains

    Gamma=[0,-1,3,-1; -1,0,-1,3; 3,-1,0,-1; -1,3,-1,0],
    det Gamma=45,
    Gamma^-1=(1/15)[2,3,7,3;3,2,3,7;7,3,2,3;3,7,3,2].

Every principal block of size>=2 is invertible; a singleton homogeneous
complementary root fails an external negative neighbor entry. Thus Gamma
is R0. At offset ones its entire strictly complementary root set is empty
support, four adjacent supports with coordinates(1,1), and four triples
with rotated coordinates(1/2,5/2,1/2). Their actual determinant indices
sum1-4+4=1; the matrix is standard-Q and has degree1. The degree-not-one
exit therefore fails. Every deleted triple has determinant6 and a negative
inverse diagonal, defeating ALL raw passive-row inverse child choices.
The positive singleton graph is an opposite matching, not a positive
three- or four-cycle, so the named singleton cyclic sources do not apply.
No new assertion about actual positive-gap membership is inferred.

For ACTUAL response quotients use F_i(q)=(1-alpha_i)Q_i-b_i, not H_a.
The necessary singleton block-row sum test on all15 partitions leaves
discrete, all-block,02|13,01|23,03|12,02|1|3,0|13|2. Each nondiscrete
partition fails actual response equality at the following exact hazard:

| blocks | q | unequal residuals |

## 39. Whole-word control: proper-clock saturation and the remaining boundary

Status: supporting ordinary UNREVIEWED mathematics. The controller value
and invariant/barrier dual are ALREADY implemented; they are not a new
producer here. The extra calculation below is an operation-level saturation
fact and a Never-face restriction, not a UE reward-class exclusion.

### 39.1 Exact source and bounded overlap check

There are n>=2 players and |r_i(S)|<=M, with M>0. The game has original
terminal rewards, live/Never0, independent private clocks, and unrestricted
complete replacements. Put s_i=r_i({i}). In the invariant reward box
Z=[-M,M]^(2n), write z=(U,B). An independent product root q acts by

    (T_q z).U_i = g_i(q)+c(q)U_i,
    (T_q z).B_i = max{Q_i(q), b_i(q)+alpha_i(q)B_i},            (W1)

where c=product_j(1-q_j), alpha_i=product_(j!=i)(1-q_j), g is
the prescribed current absorption contribution, Q is current Quit value,
and b is current opponent absorption contribution. For a chronological
word W, T_W means its backward composition. Every root and every word
is allowed; no intermediate Nash, cap bound, payoff target or regret
monotonicity is imposed. The physical seed is actual AllNever,

    e_Never=(0,s_+), s_+,i=max{0,s_i}.

The positive part is essential for signed own singletons: literal Never
earns0 against AllNever, as well as the finite Quit option earning s_i.

Finite words from this seed give ALL finite-support independent laws on
dates/Never. Their closure is the actual semantic carrier K. This exact
statement is `quittingControllerTester_reachableClosure_eq_carrier` in
`UniformEquilibrium/Quitting/ControllerTester/ControllerValue.lean`.
The target-free value and greatest USC barrier are already in
`UniformEquilibrium/Quitting/ControllerTester/FunctionBarrierDuality.lean`
and the packet
[`Controller--tester value and barrier duality`](../formalized/QUITTING_CONTROLLER_TESTER_VALUE_AND_BARRIER_DUALITY.md).
The source files were opened, including the definition
`QuittingControllerUpperSemicontinuousBarrier`, its `le_prefix` direction,
and `quittingControllerTesterFunctionBarrier_isGreatest`.
`RenewableBarrierSaturation.lean` supplies universal-prefix hull invariance
and the mandatory-Never saturation, not the proper-clock statement below.

Thus the global original-game question is the implemented one:

    eta(r)=inf_W E(T_W e_Never)=0,
    E(U,B)=max_i(B_i-U_i).                                  (W2)

The useful freedom is choosing the WHOLE word anew, including interior
clock births and intermediate cap increases. Fixed-tail repair is not
being asserted. Section37.9's cash-out example already shows why an old
tail with two proper labels can exclude every good endpoint on a solved
table, although AllNever itself admits a one-date exact equilibrium.

### 39.2 The common contraction is deleted-opponent survival

For fixed q, (W1) gives, in the common sup norm,

    ||T_q z-T_q z'|| <= (max_i alpha_i(q)) ||z-z'||.

The prescribed coefficient c is no larger than each alpha_i, and max
of a constant and an affine function is Lipschitz with the affine slope.
For a word W of length H the stronger direct bound is

    ||T_W z-T_W z'|| <= L_W ||z-z'||,
    L_W=max_i product_(t<H) alpha_i(q(t)).                    (W3)

For prescribed clocks this factor is max_i Pr(T_-i>=H). This is NOT
joint survival. If at least TWO marginal clocks are proper, then every
deleted opponent set contains a proper clock, so L_W tends to0 along
the finite prefixes. Mere a.s. joint absorption, or just ONE proper
marginal, does not suffice.

In particular the canonical word value

    Q(z)=inf_W E(T_W z)

is 2-Lipschitz on Z: E is 2-Lipschitz, all words are 1-Lipschitz,
and the infimum of uniformly Lipschitz functions has that same bound.
This improves USC regularity but, by itself, supplies NO selection
theorem. Its implemented monotonicity is

    Q(z)<=Q(T_q z),                                         (W4)

not the opposite Lyapunov direction. Distance to K has the opposite
direction, dist(T_q z,K)<=dist(z,K), simply because K is invariant.
Both facts are automatic from the original control system; neither is
evidence that eta vanishes.

### 39.3 Saturation of every root-monotone barrier

Let F be USC on the ENTIRE invariant reward box Z and suppose

    F(z)<=F(T_q z) for every z in Z and every product root q.  (W5)

Then F takes its GLOBAL MAXIMUM on every actual semantic state of a
profile having at least TWO proper marginal clocks. The same statement
holds with domain K and global maximum taken only over K. No hypothesis
on reward signs or root equilibrium is used.

Proof. If a word W has L_W<1, its map is a contraction of Z, with a
unique fixed point x_W. Iterating from any z converges to x_W, while
(W5) makes F nondecreasing. USC gives F(x_W)>=F(z) for every z. The
fixed point belongs to K because iteration from e_Never remains in K.
For the K-domain version the same argument starts from z in K.

Now let p have two proper clocks and let W_H be its first H roots.
For all sufficiently large H, L_H<1. Its actual conditional semantic
tail z_H lies in Z, and z(p)=T_(W_H) z_H. Therefore

    ||z(p)-x_(W_H)|| <=2M L_H ->0.

All x_(W_H) have the SAME global maximum of F. USC at z(p) proves the
claim. With a continuous potential satisfying the REVERSE inequality,
the same argument gives the global MINIMUM instead. This reverse version
does not use the greatest-USC-subbarrier convention.

Consequently a state potential globally monotone under every legal
prefix cannot rank good and bad components of Section37.2's discounted
source: that ENTIRE source has two proper clocks. A control argument
requiring strict monotone improvement at every bad proper-clock state
is false at this level of generality. This does not refute nonmonotone
whole-word search, Nash-restricted monotonicity, or the exported chamber
argument, whose descent is only at its constrained minimum.

There is a concrete interpretation for Q. Define

    gamma_2(r)=inf{E(p): p independent complete, with >=2 proper clocks}.

Then Q(z)=gamma_2 on EVERY actual state with two proper clocks, and
gamma_2 is the global maximum of Q on Z. Indeed prefixing such a law
preserves its two proper labels, so Q(z)>=gamma_2. Conversely Q(z)<=E(z)
on those states, and saturation makes their Q value a common constant;
taking the infimum of E gives the reverse inequality. Thus automatic
continuity does not force Q(e_Never) or gamma_2 to vanish.

### 39.4 A quantitative one-owner/Never alternative

The same contraction gives a small source-level consequence. For ANY
actual independent profile p, put z_i=Pr_p(T_i=Never) and
O=max_i product_(j!=i) z_j. Then

    E(p)>=gamma_2(r)-4M O.                                  (W6)

For prefixes with L_H<1 compare p to the periodic fixed point x_(W_H).
That fixed point is realized by a repeated word with two proper labels,
and its E is at least gamma_2. Apply E's Lipschitz bound to the preceding
2M L_H comparison and let H tend to infinity. If L_H=1 for every H,
then O=1 and (W6) is trivial since 0<=gamma_2<=2M.

Suppose additionally s_i>=s_*>0. The literal Never response W_i and
late finite deadline responses give

    B_i>=W_i+s_i product_(j!=i) z_j.

Every other prescribed own pure deadline earns at most B_i. The own
Never atom therefore forces

    B_i-U_i>=s_i product_j z_j,
    product_j z_j<=E(p)/s_*.                                (W7)

If gamma_2>0 and E(p)<=gamma_2/2, some player i consequently satisfies

    z_j>=gamma_2/(8M) for EVERY j!=i,
    z_i<=8M E(p)/(s_* gamma_2).                              (W8)

This is a literal private-clock Never-atom statement, not an unlabelled
chronological limit. It follows by choosing i with O_-i=O in(W6), noting
O>=gamma_2/(8M), and dividing(W7) by O. In particular, if a vanishing-
regret family exists while gamma_2 stays positive for this FIXED table,
after a subsequence exactly one marginal becomes almost proper and
EVERY other Never atom stays uniformly positive. Conversely gamma_2=0
already gives actual all-errors terminal approximate equilibria and the
usual fixed-target UE consumer. No existence of the family in(W8) is
being asserted.

### 39.5 Bird's-eye verdict and next exact question

The exported chamber mechanism genuinely allows global constrained
minimization without a chronological compiler. It does NOT show that
arbitrary hard data possess its raw H/J preservation or flat-support
conditions. Increasing the list of guarded reward regions is therefore
not yet a convincing end-to-end route for arbitrary Fin4.

The whole-word source here has the correct broad agency, but (W2) is
already the conjecture's exact control formulation. The new saturation
test rules out a generic globally monotone state-Lyapunov shortcut; it
does not solve the value. Selective Never is still essential: the solved
cash-out example has eta=0 while gamma_2>=1/4.

The next source-level fork is precise: either force gamma_2=0 on the
genuinely unresolved data, OR produce a low-error one-owner/Never family
when that bulk value is positive. In the second arm one must establish
the missing EXISTENCE from the raw table; (W8) only identifies the face
that an already-supplied successful family would have to approach.
Neither continuity of Q, stopping order, standard-Q, nor the present
contraction argument supplies either arm. Whole-word support changes
and intermediate regret increases remain free. No new auxiliary
correspondence, generic potential interface, export or UE-class result
is proposed here.
